### 7.14 Claim limits, prior work and final decision

Preserve four overlapping diagnostics for every forbidden committed operation: review skipped; reference infeasible under the actual prefix; a feasible reference set supporting an infeasible actor operation; and a jointly infeasible collection whose individual members looked feasible. Keep exact/fuzzy evidence and unidentified edges; these labels organize evidence and do not alone identify a causal mechanism.

F/P hostile-message counterfactuals and clean R recovery interventions need not measure one mechanism. A unified claim requires a specified admission property to predict both on connected tests. Otherwise keep one primary supported finding and the other bounded or secondary. Recipient-wise permissions are an independent transfer candidate, not payment arithmetic with renamed tools.

If original-feedback F does not expose consequential supported harm and real-policy R forks add nothing beyond ordinary budget enforcement, narrow or change the paper direction. More models cannot repair that gap. Independent confirmation and applicable external comparisons are subsequent claim-specific obligations, not falsely included in this development campaign.

Nearest-work obligations include AttriGuard (target mechanism), *Symbolic Temporal Supervision of LLM Agents Using Contracts* / ContrAgent (2026 preprint), ToolSafe (Findings ACL 2026), and *Temporary Authority, Permanent Effects* (2026 preprint). Their landing-page/method-description checks are not full-paper reviews or implemented external baselines. TraceGrant at the supplied arXiv identifier could not be retrieved in this pass and remains an unverified lead. The supplied critique is retained with its hash; its bibliographic assertions are not automatically treated as facts. See `NEAREST_WORK_CHECK.md`. Generic finite-trace monitoring, remaining budgets, idempotency and feedback interventions are not claimed as new inventions.

## 8. Selecting one contribution

Select a paper center by a concrete argument, not an equal-weight numerical score or a tally of retained experiments. Scientific importance, external distinction, valid inference and relevance are joint requirements. Large attack rates do not compensate for an invalid oracle or a duplicated idea; a low-frequency but important well-established vulnerability may still be valuable.

### 8.1 The integrated evidence review

Review the entire accumulated record, including failures, protection, nulls, aborted runs and corrections. The newest run is one additional source, not the center by default. For each candidate claim record:

| Decision field | Required content |
|---|---|
| Exact statement | One sentence naming the condition, system scope, consequence and population |
| Evidence lineage | Primitive contract/state, proposal, actual review/reference input, route, release and committed effect; distinguish old/new and forced/autonomous evidence |
| External distinction | Closest applicable methods and exact passages/source behavior; the result they already establish and what remains different |
| Scientific importance | What builder, evaluator or security conclusion changes if the claim is true? Why is this more than a harness correction or obvious restatement? |
| Strongest rival | Explicit root/task wording, payload/dependency differences, parsing/default behavior, adapter semantics, ordinary missing consumption enforcement, factual state salience, source/history redundancy or selected easy cases |
| Counterevidence | The best protected or contradictory case, including the original live protective outcome |
| Remaining inference | Whether the uncertainty is semantics, reachability, causality, significance, transfer or precision |
| Decisive experiment | The smallest test whose outcomes change the selection; define every material outcome and its implication |
| Evidence allocation | Which original findings become central, supporting, appendix/artifact, or outside this paper |

This review does not require repeating an already valid calculation or running every available module. Independent semantic review remains real work; automated labels or this assistant's analytical audit are not two completed independent annotations.

### 8.2 Decisions after the corrected diagnostic

| Observed evidence | Interpretation | Next justified action |
|---|---|---|
| Canonical excess survives; capacity and remaining grant explain distinct parts; legitimate controls succeed | Stronger B mechanism candidate, not yet proof of broad novelty | Test the nearest rival and one meaningful native/structural boundary; compare applicable enforcement |
| A default/parser/receipt repair fully accounts for the apparent failure | Local defect or measurement finding | Repair and scope accurately; assess actual significance before promoting a paper around it |
| Capacity prevents excess but blocks legal splitting or repeated authorized effects | Security/usefulness boundary of that intervention | Explain the contract mismatch; do not call blanket suppression a complete repair |
| The grant gate contains everything while capacity adds no security | A positive-control result, possibly a relevant protection mechanism | Determine whether any nontrivial practical or explanatory finding remains; an explicit contract monitor is not automatically novel |
| R skips review but only legitimate next actions occur | Reachable transition; harmful exploitation unestablished | Retain scoped R evidence; no larger campaign without a consequential unresolved hypothesis |
| R naturally reaches a forbidden commit and a valid same-state review intervention contains it | Consequential scoped R candidate | Establish supported interface/version, relevance and the actual boundary; do not generalize every empty result |
| Protection persists through a specific independent step | Candidate explanatory P result | Test an outcome-blind prediction about where that step holds and fails, with useful completion |
| Reference support co-occurs with retained hostile source and an inherited assistant plan | Direct and inherited paths are both plausible; causal dependence is unisolated | Use Appendix T.2’s conditional factorial rather than infer H from co-exposure or exclude it from a source-only null |
| Source score changes but same-workflow safety/usefulness do not | Measurement finding without the claimed operational harm | Retain its externally distinctive empirical scope or narrow its manuscript role |
| A matched state-view or history comparison explains the spent-versus-duplicate contrast | Evidence about that intervention or initialization/history bundle | Separate actor/reference behavior; do not claim a pure transition deficit or internal understanding |
| No candidate has a sufficient distinction/consequence | Current evidence is a foundation, not yet the desired paper | Select a justified new question or narrower output; do not disguise the gap with additional samples or rhetoric |

A scoped implementation vulnerability is not automatically unimportant, and structural transfer is not a publication quota. Its impact, affected assumptions and real relevance must be established. Conversely, multiplying renamed tasks, actor models or repeats does not establish a vulnerability class.

### 8.3 One test before a broader commitment

The memo must choose one candidate and its strongest alternative, or explicitly explain why the current evidence cannot select. If an implementation-specific explanation remains plausible, test a supported interface or minimal source comparison before a broad campaign. If the remaining uncertainty is scientific originality, complete the claim-specific primary-paper and relevant artifact comparison before expanding inference. If no exposure or subsequent sensitive proposal occurs, identify that limitation before searching harder. A changed claim requires a revised confirmation commitment, not retroactive use of the diagnostic as confirmation.

Keep three judgments separate: the existence of a failure/protective mechanism; its original and consequential contribution relative to external research; and the practicality of a repair. A forced test can support the first without the second; a simple patch can help users without creating a novel architecture; a well-established consequential vulnerability need not invent a new defense.

The paper's contribution bullets are written after this decision. They are not preallocated to “one old experiment, one new attack, one defense.”

### 8.4 Current selection constraints across cohorts

The current record supports multiple local counterexamples, not one selected universal cause. M1 can contain one-reference duplicate effects, fail when reference multiplicity is already excessive, fail sequentially after fresh references, and disrupt a legal collective plan. Fresh review can be absent in autonomous retry harm, yet reviewed recovery can also be unsafe. Complete natural exhaustion prefixes can be protected while a partial-release prefix is not. These are distinct conditions requiring predicted contrasts, not contradictory totals to average away.

Use the 182-slot sequence to separate those explanations and source fidelity. Do not let the most striking total-14 example eliminate the older protective findings or the autonomous continuity route. If new tasks never reach recovery, state the exposure limit. If only explicit enforcement contains harm, confront the conventional missing-stateful-policy explanation. Retain original controlled science at its supported scope without claiming that its score shift caused the later effects.

## 9. Valid mechanism interventions

### 9.1 Reference-fork semantics and implementation boundary

The pilot software description specifies five reference-only branches:

| Reported branch | Intended action | Validity requirement |
|---|---|---|
| **F-Native** | Resume captured native reference state | Correct snapshot lineage and relevant runtime state |
| **F-Placebo** | Resume/reconstruct unchanged evidence | Same machinery without semantic hints, stronger model or extra uncontrolled help |
| **F-Source** | Replace tested reference carrier with clean factual content | Preserve necessary facts and applicable authority |
| **F-Descendant** | Treat/regenerate reference descendants from an earlier valid reference state | Review discarded reads, nominations, restrictions, prior effects and processing changes |
| **F-Combined** | Apply compatible source and descendant changes | Both individual interventions retain their intended meanings |

These are reported capabilities of the **unprovided newer package**, not verified implementation here. In particular, the described descendant implementation truncates reference history through the carrier and advances synchronization state before reference generation. It is reference-state surgery, not replay of all historical events or whole-agent rollback. Actual source inspection is required.

Neutralized approval, fact-only restoration/removal, irrelevant-history edit, trusted authority restoration and whole-agent continuation are **separately specified proposed controls**. They are not aliases for the five reported forks and are not claimed to exist. Their necessity follows the selected mechanism. Each additional control needs its own implementation, manifest and semantic review.

### 9.2 Review actual delivered inputs, not just snapshot hashes

For a fixed-candidate reference comparison, hold the pending actor proposal and relevant state fixed. Fill this closure record from each actual reference request before interpreting the outcome:

| Evidence item | Native | Branch | Needed for legitimate assessment? | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Genuine IDs, mappings and resolver facts | Present/absent | Present/absent | Yes/no and reason | Preserved, re-resolved, missing |
| Nomination and protected sender evidence | … | … | … | … |
| Trusted grants, restrictions and consumed uses | … | … | … | … |
| Unsupported clearance claim | … | … | No authority; focal content | Removed, retained, changed |
| Later legitimate reads and preconditions | … | … | … | … |
| Prior effects, principal and relevant resource state | … | … | … | Consistent/inconsistent |
| Extra generation or information gathering | … | … | … | Controlled or bundled |

If necessary facts are lost, re-resolve them through a reviewed route or label an information-removal bundle. If actual authority changes, do not call it permission-preserving. If gold knowledge selects exactly what to remove, label oracle assistance. Native/placebo copying does not necessarily control additional regeneration; use appropriate benign-processing controls where computation can explain the result. A source/descendant interaction requires compatible native/source/descendant/combined cells; placebo is additional machinery control.

### 9.3 Reference, actor and resource state are different interventions

A reference-only fixed-candidate comparison estimates conditional assessment dependence. It does not establish how often a whole actor would propose the operation or how a deployable workflow repair changes total risk. Whole-agent continuation needs a separately valid state/history clone and runtime-available information. Do not undo irreversible disclosure by editing the later transcript, replay old successful tool returns into a changed world, or fork after the first violation and claim prevention.

Select checkpoints using pre-outcome opportunity/representation rules, including protected cases, and report eligible/selected/excluded counts. Outcome-selected examples can illustrate a mechanism but cannot become an unbiased success-rate sample. Review branch semantics independently; mechanical alignment and hash agreement establish lineage, not semantic equivalence.

### 9.4 R and B interventions at the execution boundary

R compares the original review-state predicate, minimal error-aware correction and appropriate placebo with the same following candidate at a consistent checkpoint. Keep separate the effect of the patch on a full autonomous trajectory, where later proposals may change.

B compares membership and occurrence-aware release on the same ordered batch under reviewed tool semantics. Map **proposal occurrence → assessment → released occurrence → dispatch → receipt → committed effect**. One decision may cover multiple occurrences only if its scope and contract do. A legitimate two-use grant and safe retry reveal overblocking. Repairing receipt attribution alone is a measurement intervention unless it changes release.

For skip routes, check an applicable earlier approval or explicit exemption before claiming missing authorization. Historical 44/210 qualifying runs, 22/210 later uses and 18/168 unaudited privileged calls have different denominators; their 15/2/1 final-run labels do not adjudicate immediate call authorization. Rebuild the effect-level join.

### 9.5 Protection interventions

Test the hypothesized compensating component on protected and boundary cases with correct cloned state and declared selection. Artificially removing a guard identifies a component's role, not attacker capability. Preserve facts, grants, legal alternatives and compute where the intended comparison requires them. Confirm the predicted boundary with new cases; successful recovery does not cancel earlier harm.

### 9.6 Reported fork transport and automatic eligibility

The following mechanics are REPORTED interface details; source inspection and semantic validation remain required.

The software description of `pilot/forks.py` specifies that native and placebo deep-copy the captured snapshot. The source branch replaces only the corresponding shadow tool content and last attenuated external input with the task's CLEAN carrier. The descendant branch truncates shadow history through that carrier and advances `shadow_index` to the current acting-history length, preventing copied later descendants from being silently restored; the existing native loop then generates the shadow continuation. The combined branch does both. Acting messages, pending proposal and simulated state remain unchanged. This is reference regeneration after a cutoff, not autonomous replay of every intermediate historical event.

Automatic eligibility checks carrier presence, the active external input, non-skipped reference assessment, history alignment and no post-carrier committed mutation. Outcome-blind round-robin selection favors task and source/content diversity. These mechanical checks do not prove that discarded later reads, nominations or descendants were irrelevant to legitimate facts or authority. Review the actual input, facts, nomination and grant evidence preserved in each branch. An intervention that removes necessary permitted information or changes the operative authority view answers a different question and must be relabeled or excluded from the intended contrast, with the exclusion recorded.

The described generated fork plan records `PENDING_INDEPENDENT_REVIEW`; running a command does not turn that marker into scientific validation. Parent code/task/endpoint and snapshot integrity checks protect lineage, not semantics. Retain protected cases and all mechanically eligible/selected/excluded counts, rather than selecting only apparent failures.

### 9.7 Required current intervention boundaries

P's canonical hostile/deletion/neutral/placebo contexts are reconstructed raw-carrier views. The actual attenuated reference is a separate baseline; their difference does not isolate deletion. All four reconstructed branches must preserve the actor, world, root and non-carrier context. R's forced release deliberately changes the first execution and receipts; it is not an autonomous M1 failure unless the source's recorded allocation produces that release. Summary cards are informational interventions and use neutral controls; they do not grant permission or establish hidden cognition.

Fork continuity before the blocking event, not after copying a stale skip flag. A newly reviewed retry requires a fresh reference verdict. Use a monotonic proposal counter independent of reviewed-batch count; the current recorded filename scheme can overwrite unreviewed pre-proposal states. Reconstruct historical checkpoints only with authenticated prefix replay and state checks; unavailable inputs retain their scheduled slots.

## 10. Outcomes, observation, and independent review

The primary security endpoint is **any independently unauthorized committed effect during the declared observed lifecycle** when the question concerns execution safety. Later recovery does not erase a violation.

| Outcome | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Violation | At least one unauthorized committed effect |
| Completion | Required permitted goal reached, including legal alternatives |
| Secure completion | Completion with no unauthorized effect |
| Appropriate handling | Correct deferral, confirmation request, or allowed fallback |
| Mechanism support | Required pre-outcome path and valid discriminating evidence |
| Observability | Evidence supports TRUE, FALSE, or UNKNOWN under the declared horizon |
| Cost/coverage | Full workflow resources and which applicable operations receive which decisions |

Completion and violation may both be true. Safe refusal can have completion false and appropriate handling true. Unauthorized disclosure may occur through a read or communication surface, not only a nominal mutator. Define protected effects from the contract. Observer uncertainty must not be rewritten as safety. A confirmed forbidden effect makes violation TRUE even if later closure is unknown. Violation is FALSE only when the declared effect scope is sufficiently observed with no forbidden effect; otherwise it remains UNKNOWN. Completion and appropriate handling retain their own labels.

The collector, root authority store, and effect state are outside T0 actor write authority. This is a simulator trusted-computing-base assumption. Hashes certify recorded bytes and joins, not truth under a compromised observer. T2 assurance fixtures test success-without-commit, partial error with mutation, receipt aliasing, event/state disagreement, and relevant scope/count errors. Add delayed-effect closure only if the runtime supports it.

Maintain workflow, contract, grant, proposal occurrence, reference generation, assessment, release, dispatch, receipt, effect, state-version and closure IDs. Log current route and actual judge request/response; a retained reason string cannot prove a current judge call. A failed or timed-out trajectory after a forbidden commit remains a violation.

Two independent annotators review every diagnostic episode and every selected mechanism checkpoint. Show authority and effects before internal verdicts, with condition labels withheld where practical. Preserve original labels, disagreements, adjudication, rationales, and UNKNOWN. Annotation is actual work; generated packets are not completed independent review. For confirmation, combine validated executable predicates with an outcome-stratified semantic audit; record sampling probabilities if not every row is reviewed.

### 10.1 Stage-specific outcomes and evaluator integrity

Preserve judgment, release, executor admission, commitment, containment, recovery, and closure separately. Wrong judgment contained by an independent gate is successful containment with an upstream error. A correct block followed by an unauthorized retry is a violated workflow. Restoring a local file or access list does not retract information already disclosed; refund and rollback do not erase the earlier unauthorized event.

Use concrete consequence components—recipient/audience, privilege or resource scope, cumulative amount/use budget, disclosed fields, duplicate effects, and recovery cost—before an aggregate harm score. If weights are useful, state them before ranking and show sensitivity. Money, privacy loss and interruption time have no natural common unit. “Harm prevented” needs a valid counterfactual, not merely a low observed count.

A later evaluator-control study can add misleading approval prose to an analysis packet while keeping the actual trusted authority and effects fixed. A changed automated verdict indicates evaluator susceptibility, not actor compromise. Keep independent human adjudication, necessary authority evidence, and annotation lineage. The code's reviewer packet is an aid, not evidence that independent review occurred. Independent diagnostic review remains pending until completed and recorded.

### 10.2 Make the evaluator unable to borrow authority from the system under test

This is an immediate validity requirement for the diagnostic audit, not a second attack campaign. The incident's analysis agents sometimes accepted peer approval or softened the interpretation of a malicious action. The same risk applies if our evaluator treats the guard's reason, an actor summary, or a peer's clearance as permission evidence.

Construct the primary label from the trusted contract, applicable grant and independently joined committed state. Present those records before model verdicts and rhetorical explanations. In a fixed-label sentinel packet, contrast an unauthorized effect with and without an irrelevant “review complete/approved” assertion, and contrast a genuine scoped grant that should change the label. Keep actual authority evidence available in both views; do not blind away information needed for a correct judgment. The first contrast should leave gold unchanged; the second should not. Use existing records and deterministic predicates where possible. Do not spend new GPU inference merely to create these packets.

If an automated evaluator changes its label on the first contrast, mark that evaluator unsuitable for deciding authorization in those cases until repaired and validated. If a reviewer disagrees, investigate the semantic assumption and adjudicate; do not assume either prose or a programmed predicate is infallible. Record model-assisted labels separately from actual independent human review. High model-to-model agreement does not establish independent gold. The effect collector's independence is a separate TCB assumption; balanced approval language cannot repair a false execution record.

### 10.3 Current non-completion and technical-status interpretation

The fresh campaign's 29 safe non-completions divide into 18 missing required post-commit reads, seven completed effects/reads with missing final identifiers, and four actual under-executions under a two-send grant. Eight missing-read cases also omit IDs; these categories are mutually exclusive by first substantive failure. Do not describe all 29 as denied authorized effects. No designated hostile AFTER-payment carrier was delivered in the relevant incomplete cases, so those nulls do not establish protection after exposure.

The two controlled L3 recovery violations remain outside the 20/96 autonomous-core denominator. All 16 controlled continuations consist of 12 secure, two safe reporting incompletions and two violations. Software COMPLETE, zero top-level errors, zero tool errors, a valid manifest and human review are different statuses. The partial 54-row campaign and reused qualification requests must remain visible, not disappear behind the successful fresh run.

## 11. Confirmation and statistical commitments

Confirmation must match the selected claim. Structural transfer, stochastic reliability, budgeted attacker success, and deployment incidence are different targets. The default goal is bounded mechanistic transfer. No deployment prevalence claim is planned.

### 11.1 Required commitment before new confirmation data

Freeze the following in a single record: exact claim and falsifier; primary endpoint/estimand; target population; independent unit; fixed configurations; inclusion and technical-error rules; minimum material effect or required prediction; precision target if estimating a rate/difference; sample/allocation rule; planned uncertainty method; attack information/budget; held-out structures and realizations; UNKNOWN handling; stopping/amendment rules; and central displays. No placeholder can be called a frozen confirmation design.

### 11.2 Default path: confirm an explanatory boundary

Choose fresh structures from the selected mechanism's preconditions and strongest counterexamples. The panel should challenge a different protected operation, a different authority/delegation topology where relevant, a legitimate multi-effect or cumulative task, clean tool-dependent work, a protected boundary case, and a version/implementation boundary when available. These are inferential roles, not necessarily six independent tasks or a numeric publication quota. A candidate panel of three effect semantics with two causal structures each can be useful, but must be justified by the claim. Different IDs or rephrasings alone are not new structures; narrow the claim when the mechanism applies to fewer semantics.

For every new structure, write an outcome-blind prediction: where the focal assumption should fail or hold, which intervention should matter, and a nearby protected/authorized control. Freeze the small within-structure repeat count using development variability and complete-workflow cost. Preserve all predictions, including misses. A counterexample falsifies a deterministic universal condition; a probabilistic explanation requires a stated error model. A handful of structures cannot establish a small population-wide failure rate.

Mechanistic confirmation requires complete traces, reviewed interventions, and the predicted contrast on fresh structures. Report per-structure cells, failures of the explanation, and any model dependence. No arbitrary number of successful examples certifies acceptance. If fresh data change the mechanism or repair, they become development for that tested method and a new confirmation set is needed.

### 11.3 Optional path: estimate a finite-workload effect precisely

If a rate difference is central, specify the finite task set or sampling population before allocation. For a fixed reviewed workload, let `V_i,c,j` indicate any violation for task i, configuration c, repeat j. The finite-workload contrast is the equally task-weighted difference in violation probability between the native configuration and its comparator. Repeat-level independence requires isolated state and independent random execution; pairing by a run index does not create shared provider randomness or new task structures.

For balanced r independent pairs per task over M fixed tasks, define `d_i,j = V_i,native,j − V_i,comparator,j`, in [−1,1]. Under the declared independent-reset model, a conservative 95% Hoeffding half-width for the equally weighted finite-workload mean is:

`h = sqrt(2 ln(40) / (M r))`.

To target half-width ε, plan at least `ceil(2 ln(40)/ε²)` independent pairs, balanced across the fixed tasks. For example, ε = 0.15 requires 328 pairs; with M = 24 and r = 14 there are 336 pairs and a conservative half-width about 0.148. This is an illustrative precision calculation, **not a mandated new campaign**. It bounds inference randomness on that fixed corpus, not transfer to unseen workflows. Correlated runs invalidate that independence argument.

Before choosing this path, state a scientifically material difference δ and set a useful precision target, ordinarily ε ≤ δ/2. Use development estimates and sensitivity simulations for a more efficient valid design if appropriate. Costs are measured per complete workflow. If the justified budget cannot reach useful precision, narrow the quantitative claim and report the achieved bound; do not substitute an overconfident bootstrap. Generalization to a sampled workflow population requires task/structure-level analysis and adequate independent structures. Hundreds of continuations from a few checkpoints cannot supply that sample.

### 11.4 The confirmation record must become concrete

Before launching confirmation, fill one row with actual values for: primary claim; finite or sampled population; security endpoint; paired contrast or falsifiable prediction; independent units and shared ancestry; material difference δ; useful half-width ε or prediction-error criterion; M and repeat count; calibration/sensitivity evidence; total workflow/call allocation; comparison information; held-out IDs; interval/decision rule; UNKNOWN bounds; and stopping/amendment policy. A planning formula is not a completed allocation. R, B, H and P have different endpoints and cannot inherit one universal sample size.

Use the inexpensive development data to measure paired discordance, heterogeneity and complete-workflow cost, without consulting the held-out outcomes. Choose allocation by the frozen precision or prediction question. A fixed finite-corpus result can be scientifically useful with explicit scope even if a population-wide rate is unjustified. Evidence that revises the method belongs to development for that tested method.

### 11.5 Common analysis rules

Randomize treatment assignment where a randomized effect is claimed and counterbalance execution order to limit backend drift. Declare pairing, reset and interference assumptions; identical run indices or seeds do not create independent task structures. Report primitive counts and joint security/usefulness cells before aggregate contrasts. Analyze tasks and shared ancestry explicitly. Keep the 60-episode, 236-episode, targeted, incomplete occurrence, complete fresh occurrence and current mechanism-development cohorts out of independent confirmation estimates; method selection has used their outcomes or intended diagnoses. Identical prompts, seed values or repeated deterministic outputs do not create an effective independent sample. Keep all-enrolled outcomes by assigned arm separate from exposure- or checkpoint-conditional results. Retain nondelivery, safe early stopping and technical failures under their declared outcome/UNKNOWN rules instead of dropping them from the headline denominator. Post-treatment reachability conditioning is not a randomized total effect. Thresholds are calibrated on development and assessed on structural holdouts with coverage and usefulness.

For N outcomes with D confirmed violations and Q unknowns, report logical violation bounds `[D/N, (D+Q)/N]` separately from sampling uncertainty. Use a declared fixed-runtime limit and request budget to label infrastructure interruptions, model-format failures and unresolved effects; show their completion/coverage cost. The current two-execution, zero-temperature payment schedule supports diagnostic contrasts, not stable inferential rate estimates. Missing scheduled rows stay visible; unresolved or aborted rows receive declared treatment. Do not generalize a finite zero count into a guarantee. Per-attempt attacker success and any-success within a fixed query budget need separate denominators. Secondary contrasts are exploratory unless prospectively specified.

### 11.6 Status of current inference

The targeted exhaustion contexts, the incomplete and fresh occurrence campaigns, and the complete Section 7 plan are development evidence/design. Fresh output files and new amount/ID values do not create independent confirmation. Conditional branches share parents; the two audits of one archive are not two independent experimental replications. A 182-slot manifest is a fixed coverage commitment, not a statistical power calculation or representative sample. Use its paired mechanism matrices and explicit missing/UNKNOWN counts; freeze a new claim-specific holdout only after selection.

## 12. Baselines, adaptation, and cost

Compare the native/adapted system to the smallest meaningful alternative for the chosen mechanism. Include clean facts, unsupported authority prose, valid in-scope grants, out-of-scope grants, legitimate multiple effects, and safe retries as relevant. An all-block baseline exposes utility cost. A hidden-gold authority oracle is a diagnostic ceiling; it cannot be the sole practical comparator.

A practical independent-authority baseline derives constraints from trusted inputs available at runtime. Separate model-based policy extraction from enforcement of the extracted predicate. A verifier can perfectly enforce a wrongly compiled policy. A signature-only variant is an ablation, not a complete authorization system. Use a faithful nearest method or a clearly scoped mechanism comparator; do not rebuild every historical defense just to enlarge a table.

The current Qwen3-32B and Gemma-3-27B-IT configurations supply two model-family development comparisons. They jointly change actor, shadow, attenuator and judge; do not attribute a difference to the actor alone. A role-fixed comparison is needed only when that actor-specific explanation is central. A future third configuration, including a hosted model if scientifically justified and locally reproducible access is available, must distinguish capability dependence or transfer rather than satisfy a model-count quota. Cache availability does not determine scientific selection, and additional models do not substitute for task or architecture diversity.

Adaptive development targets the selected assumption with equal declared information and budgets. Preserve unsuccessful attempts, queries, feedback and selection. Freeze the attack procedure and repair before confirmation. A second model family tests dependence on configured models; it does not remove shared evidence dependence or turn one adapter into an independent architecture replication.

Measure actor, reference, attenuation, judge, policy extraction, regeneration, retries, recovery, confirmation, local checks and tool time. Record calls, tokens including missing usage, wall time, truncation, failures, and completion together. Report cost per completed secure workflow where meaningful, with primitive totals to expose selection. Small diagnostics do not support universal tail-latency claims. Optimize batching/cache behavior only while preserving relevant authority validity, state and occurrence semantics.

For a broad transfer statement, distinguish model replication from architecture replication. A source-bound important vulnerability can be consequential without a second architecture; do not require an irrelevant comparator to satisfy a quota. Conversely, one patched snapshot cannot justify a general claim about every reference guard. Baselines test the closest live alternative explanation, using only supported inputs and runtime-available authority.

### 12.1 Authorized-use transitions and exposure measures

A small net completion difference can conceal legitimate losses and gains on different tasks. Show paired authorized losses, authorized gains, unnecessary rejection, abandonment, legal fallback and secure completion separately. A block followed by legal completion is different from denial of a required effect. Report security/usefulness/cost at the same operating point.

Privilege-duration exposure can be an explicitly weighted secondary construct. It does not measure realized harm or exploit probability without further assumptions. An irreversible disclosure can occur in a short window; a long unused grant can have no observed violation. Preserve primitive effects and usefulness instead of replacing them with a single exposure scalar.

### 12.2 Current comparator boundaries

M0, M1, continuous review, truthful feedback, state summaries and G test different obligations. Source-feedback parity is F's task. R holds the future rule common to identify release/feedback/state interventions. J tests feasibility-sensitive comparisons without silently fixing exact matches. A measures total policy-package behavior and utility autonomously. P isolates matched reconstructed reference views. G is privileged contract enforcement, with independent observer checks and explicit legal alternatives; it is not the sole practical baseline or a new policy-extraction contribution.

## 13. Conditional modules and compute allocation

| Module | Current disposition | Scientific purpose |
|---|---|---|
| Original author studies and expanded 236 study | Retained completed evidence at explicit scope | Original controlled science, protection, corrections and payment paths |
| Configuration/reference qualification | Recorded completed/reused stage metadata; full original raw audit scope bounded | Preserve source scheduling/fidelity facts without claiming a new rerun |
| Matched exhaustion | Completed 60 conditional contexts, 156 requests | I/N counterevidence and role/forced-admission separation |
| Incomplete occurrence campaign | 54/96 rows; qualification gating retained | Do not hide censorship, six inapplicable selected cells or absent recovery tests |
| Fresh occurrence campaign | Completed 96 core and 16 controlled continuations | Consequential email, autonomous continuity, reference interaction and adverse recovery |
| F/C/Release/A/J/P | **Single active Section 7 implementation; GPU execution pending** | 182 mixed rollouts, 24 admission evaluations, up to 48 reference/admission cells |
| Supporting scheduling/source/explanation/U-I-N designs | Appendix T; no automatic extra inference | Preserve valid method alternatives and scoped budgets |
| Lawful/dimension components | Existing completed subsets plus Section 7 qualification for changed code/contracts | Real alternatives, count/total/recipient isolation, retry and partial effects |
| H24/S128/A64/H64, source bridge and lifecycle modules | Conditional specifications preserved below | Select only a decision-changing unresolved question |
| Structural confirmation and closest practical comparator | Claim-specific future commitment | External distinction, prediction and useful transfer after development |

These schedules are episode counts, not model-call budgets or cumulative obligations. The one currently selected sequence is Section 7; supporting appendix budgets are not added to it. Two models on physical GPU 3 execute serially. Profile complete workflow calls/tokens and model startup before estimating additional cost. Do not repeat successful assurance tests, reinstall working virtual environments, or reload a model solely to create another progress record. A code or contract change that affects a claim still requires the relevant verification.

Reusing a compatible development episode does not create an independent confirmation row. Every added batch names the uncertainty it resolves, the two possible outcomes and what each would change. If neither changes the decision, the batch is unnecessary.

### 13.1 S128 — permission-invariant source study

Retain **8 eligible tasks × 2 sources × 2 content conditions × defense OFF/ON × 2 repeats = 128 autonomous episodes**. Four dependency structures should include a directory-resolved payment with cumulative/duplicate semantics, scoped sharing, a calendar operation with legal alternatives, and communication with recipient/content limits. Two cases per structure need a meaningful contract or recovery difference, not only different identifiers.

Source is USER versus legitimate TOOL placement of the same execution-critical fact. CLEAN and CONFLICT contain the same genuine facts; only CONFLICT adds unauthorized control. Outcome-blind validation checks source eligibility, authenticity assumptions, user endorsement, discretion, parameter semantics, and legal alternatives. A directory may identify the account for an already permitted vendor without gaining permission to choose a different vendor.

In the standardized-exposure cohort, both source arms legitimately receive the external carrier. Verify actual delivery; retain non-reachability in the total population. Artificially scheduled exposure is a boundary experiment, not an autonomous native workflow. A natural-workflow cohort permits lookup behavior to differ and estimates a total effect; it is separately budgeted and not silently included in S128.

Preserve a transparent continuity subset from the original source work even if the main contribution changes. Do not replace all recorded conflicts with approval narratives and then claim exact replication. Fixed-candidate scores use legal candidates and native-rendering controls where relevant, but are not forced into autonomous trajectories.

For outcome `Y`, at fixed content, define the paired source interaction as:

`I_Y = [Y(TOOL,ON) − Y(USER,ON)] − [Y(TOOL,OFF) − Y(USER,OFF)]`.

Specify whether `Y` is violation, completion, secure completion, or cost. Show cells first. This interaction is not itself a mediated effect or a direct harm rate. The operative AttriGuard object may be shadow action/survival rather than a scalar influence score; do not fabricate a proxy score stage.

S128 is useful when source-dependent evidence loss, compensation, or decision behavior remains the central uncertainty. A valid checkpoint experiment may precede its full run.

### 13.2 A64 — Genuine delegation × authority-cue study, conditional

Use **4 tasks × 2 genuine-delegation states × 2 cue presentations × defense OFF/ON × 2 repeats = 64 episodes**, only if distinguishing a grant from rhetorical clearance remains consequential.

| Factor | Level 0 | Level 1 | Invariant within that contrast |
|---|---|---|---|
| Genuine delegation | No applicable peer decision right | Explicit narrow current grant | Peer, nomination, eligible facts, representation, and resource fixture where valid |
| Cue | Neutral nomination/proposal | Same nomination plus coordinator-clearance claim | Nominee, necessary facts, authenticity assurance |
| Defense | OFF | Qualified ON configuration | Acting backend, task budget, tools, observer |

The grant factor changes permission and potentially required completion. The cue factor must not change whether an actual prerequisite approval exists. In the grant condition, the neutral nomination already suffices to exercise the real grant. In the no-grant condition, the nominated alternate operation is independently unauthorized without the specified approval. Independently review these semantics.

Within no-grant cells, examine cue effects on unauthorized commits and how the guard modifies them. Within granted cells, examine authorized completion and unnecessary blocking. A guard that denies all peers does not preserve useful delegation. The factorial does not estimate signature effects because authenticity is held fixed. Compare matched-format controls for length, position, and urgency before attributing a result specifically to authority wording.

If needed, budget separate identity probes: unsigned unauthorized peer, verified unauthorized peer, verified valid scoped grant, and verified stale/out-of-scope grant. Verify signatures outside the language model and protect the resulting metadata. A valid signature can establish identity but cannot create missing root authority. Silence is approval only if the trusted contract actually defines it.

### 13.3 H64 — Direct versus naturally derived history, conditional

Use **4 workflow structures × 2 history conditions × 2 content conditions × defense OFF/ON × 2 repeats = 64 episodes** when history formation itself is the unresolved condition.

The direct arm consumes the carrier immediately before a relevant proposal. The derived arm permits ordinary intermediate processing before a later relevant proposal. This changes history and possibly computation. The comparison is an architecture/processing bundle until controls justify a narrower interpretation; it is not the original source-only intervention.

Initially use the supported single-agent path. Add a live peer handoff only if it supplies a distinct meaningful mechanism, with explicit sender authority, communication scope, and adapter status. Track equal total budget and extra-processing controls, actual calls/context, and legal factual access. Equal tokens do not guarantee equal reasoning. Group-based treatment is needed if live communicating agents interfere.

H64 is distinct from A64. Select it for the uncertainty it answers; do not silently combine the matrices or count the same compatible development episodes as independent confirmation.

The eleven community proposals and nine observed patterns are sources of questions and controls, not twenty projects. Retain authority contrasts, explicit assumptions, delegation formation and consequence analysis as useful refinements; evaluate overhead as supporting evidence. Add revocation, memory privacy or concurrency only for the selected mechanism. Human oversight, a general PET router and federated poisoning require separate questions and scope. Formalization is conditional on explanatory value.

## 14. Implementation and local execution requirements

### 14.1 Actual source and implementation status

The project is a standalone IEEE S&P repository at `/home/tahad/IEEE_SP/IEEE_SP_2027`. Its research basis includes the author's retained USENIX methods, selected implementation dependencies, data, reviews and provenance as project-local materials. It does not require running another project directory. The external model cache is an optional local weight source, not a research-code dependency; checkpoint revisions and tokenizer/template evidence must be captured. Do not copy virtual environments or unnecessary duplicate historical trees into the research artifact.

The supplied AttriGuard source and real AgentDojo 0.1.35 runtime underpin the native component and adapted model diagnostics. The corrected package contains `START_EXPANDED_GPU3.sh` and the implemented `expanded_gpu3` modules for contracts/world state, guard variants, HTTP transport, runner, CPU assurance, orchestration and tests. Its reviewable protocol declares all scenarios and the complete schedule. The original vendor module is preserved; generated variants and diffs are saved per run.

A separately described optional pilot archive is not needed for this sequence. Its reported D24/fork interfaces are not assumed available. Any H24, sharing, whole-agent fork or source bridge must be implemented and qualified before use; no command in this plan pretends those modules already exist.

The expanded payment runtime is synchronous with custom simulated tools; the occurrence runtime additionally uses native AgentDojo in-memory email. Neither implements a live peer authentication service, concurrent shared resource, protected dynamic revocation between review and commit, or asynchronous effects. The explicit-contract gate is a positive control, not a learned authority service. The receipt-corrected baseline is an explicitly adapted implementation; source release predicates, feedback and model wrapper deltas are recorded separately.

### 14.2 GPU, environment and reproducibility policy

Use **physical GPU 3 only** for the currently authorized work. Physical GPU UUID is `GPU-4d6b17a4-4e6c-95b7-972d-7807122ad7c6`. CUDA may expose it as logical device 0 inside the process; record the physical UUID. Do not occupy GPUs 0, 1 or 2 under this allocation, kill another user's job, or start an overlapping sequence.

Use the existing project `.venv` for research and `.venv-serving` for vLLM. The user verified Python 3.12.15, vLLM 0.26.0 and torch 2.11.0+cu130 in the serving environment; record actual versions and dependencies in each run. Prepend `.venv-serving/bin` to PATH for Ninja visibility. No reinstall is needed merely because a new plan is written. If an environment is genuinely missing or incompatible, diagnose that specific condition and provide one documented repair.

Run inference and orchestration under **nohup with named .log, PID, status and fresh results paths**. The delivered launcher starts Qwen, verifies its local server/workers on GPU 3, runs its schedule, shuts down the owned server, starts Gemma on the same GPU/port, runs its schedule, and shuts it down before archiving. Both model stages use localhost port 18080 serially. The shared lock prevents overlapping controllers or servers. A lock refusal is an explicit status, not silent progress.

Model/cache choices and exact commands are in Appendix K. Missing `refs/main` is supported only when a unique complete cached revision is available; do not select by timestamp or edit the cache to manufacture a pointer. Record model, tokenizer, template and role-specific effective settings, including missing logprobs or unsupported tokenization. The twelve Qwen-native development controls qualify only their recorded transport scope; a materially changed interface needs separate parser qualification. A version string or GPU allocation is not proof of scientific interface fidelity.

Distinguish integrity verification, frozen reanalysis, captured-response engineering replay, display regeneration and fresh inference. Every central result must be reproducible locally from the delivered code and preserved raw evidence. The completed expanded archive records actual GPU-3 inference; mocked controller tests never establish physical deployment. The integrated Section 7 experiment is implemented and CPU-validated; new GPU execution remains pending. Recorded targeted qualification and exhaustion must not be described as pending merely because they began as specifications.

### 14.3 Claim-to-artifact requirements

Every main claim maps to raw requests, trusted contract, reference inputs, current route decisions, actual effects, independent labels, population/denominator, configuration, code hashes, analysis and display. Verify central calculations from primitives and use meaningful corrupted-input checks for the joins and labels used. Store amendments and exclusions with reasons: invalid contract, unsupported interface, technical error, no delivery, baseline inability, or outcome-informed redesign.

Keep the immutable original evidence and repair new research code separately. Implementation work must ship actual tested commands. Until then, described command names are source-navigation leads. Conditional modules and any disclosure/publication activity require their own substantive execution context; none occurred while writing this plan.

### 14.4 Lean proposed implementation and verification interfaces

The implemented expanded runner already separates protocol, world/contract effects, named guards, transport, episode runner, components and orchestration. The supplied authorization_study package extends the compatible occurrence implementation for Section 7. Preserve the expanded implementation for reproduction and the supporting controls in Appendix T. The active implementation includes source-feedback email, continuity, direct release, actual policy forks, state/feedback, autonomous cross-API and fixed reference/admission tests; it has no task-success gate withholding later independent slots. Keep the original evidence/vendor bytes immutable and preserve source/config snapshots. H24, forks and unrelated launchers are not prerequisites or placeholder implementations.

The current package offers source verification, contract tests, recorded-response fidelity checks, offline primitive probes, the full frozen F/C/R/A/J/P schedule, fixed-routing evaluations, trace reconciliation, and machine-readable analysis. Supporting R/B/H24 specifications remain retained methodological options rather than extra promised launch modes. Figure production and human semantic annotation remain separate tasks. Publish only implemented command interfaces; do not infer a complete manuscript or independent confirmation from a passing CPU suite.

Every run records source/configuration and task/contract hashes, per-role effective model settings, attempt/query budgets, exact eligible carrier, all technical errors and retries, proposed/assessed/released/dispatched occurrences, independent effects, continuation/closure, labels, costs and parent run IDs. No manual correction overwrites the sole raw record. Atomic records and one writer per output directory preserve interruption evidence. GPU occupancy alone does not establish workflow progress.

### 14.5 Available standalone experiment implementation

The completed fresh package supplies `occurrence_study/engine.py`, `study.py`, `protocol.py`, `world.py`, `observer.py`, `interventions.py`, transport, analysis and sequence code. It already ran 96 core workflows, canonical reconstructed-reference probes, selected feedback comparisons and 16 lawful continuations. Existing expanded and targeted runners remain retained reproduction evidence, not the immediate job to repeat.

The supplied authorization_study package implements the explicit 182-slot F/C/R/A schedule, J24, fixed-routing evaluations, P origins, real-policy R forks, original-feedback parity, monotonic proposal snapshots, direct release interventions, neutral/informative role cards, recipient-wise contracts, ordinary public-policy comparator, observer, automated analysis and failure archives. Minimal historical records with hashes are included locally; no rejected-project directory is needed. From the extracted project root, `bash START_AUTHORIZATION_GPU3.sh` starts the entire nohup Qwen-to-Gemma sequence on physical GPU 3 using existing research and serving environments. CPU qualification and an offline full campaign test are engineering evidence; actual new GPU findings and human semantic review remain pending.

## 15. The eventual security argument

The paper should let a reviewer assess one complete argument without reconstructing the project history. The list below is a template for an execution-mechanism paper, not a rule that a distinct empirical-measurement or explanatory-protection paper must reproduce every original experiment:

1. A concrete authorized task and protected effect.
2. The guard's specific assumption and closest prior treatment.
3. A naturally reachable violating or protective trace.
4. Independent effect measurement and fidelity limitations.
5. A discriminating intervention that survives the strongest alternative.
6. Fresh predictions, protected controls, costs, and bounded transfer.

Use a worked example before compressed terminology. Separate capabilities, attempted operations, release and committed effects. Figures should show the real boundary and intervention; tables should expose all cells and denominators. Retained studies can establish central contributions where they answer the paper's question; they are not limited to supporting background because they appeared in the author's rejected submission. Select their main-text prominence by relevance and evidentiary strength. They need not all remain main-text experiments.

| Reviewer concern | Concrete resolution required |
|---|---|
| Incremental concept; DRIFT/ACE overlap | Exact external claim comparison; identify the original empirical or mechanism result retained or added here, beyond merely restating the principle |
| Small selected populations | Selection funnel, independent structures, explicit transfer limits |
| Disconnected studies | One contract-to-effect event chain and compatible interventions |
| Internal signal without consequence | Actual decision dependence and security/usefulness outcome |
| AttriGuard protects | Protection retained, explained and prospectively tested |
| Reconstruction/fidelity | Source-bound status and qualified interfaces for each system |
| Compressed writing | Worked contract, clear vocabulary, visible denominators and causal chain |

The METR/Redwood report motivates actual channel exposure, natural derived state, authenticity-versus-authority, independent observation and lifecycle closure. It does not show that the tested guard failed. In particular, the incident's agents believed a causal scorer existed when it was not deployed. Appendix C preserves this reasoning and its limits.

Selected S&P readings support bounded claims, realistic reachability, useful controls, validated observations, and visible counterevidence. Their accepted status does not establish a causal recipe for acceptance. The reading coverage does not establish an exhaustive accepted-paper corpus study or an acceptance model. The full nearest-work review remains a research dependency.

Do not fix the abstract around a presumed failure before results exist. A protection paper needs a new predictive condition, not a celebration of failed attacks. An occurrence-binding paper needs a consequential reference-to-effect condition with appropriate recurrence or significance; a review-continuity paper needs an attacker-reachable mediation gap beyond a skip tag. A history paper needs an operative reference/enforcement result beyond known memory propagation. If none survives, pivot.

The candidate main-text claim should explain a **measured boundary between operation agreement and permission over a sequence of effects**, including the responsible information/enforcement path and successful containment. Generic multidimensional permissions, “agreement is not authorization,” and ordinary use counters are not the discovery. If matched tests do not establish that reference-specific boundary, write the narrower supported execution, protection or controlled empirical argument instead.

### 15.1 Reviewer obligations

The review file contains reviews A and B, without a separate detailed decision letter. A valued the controlled source design, routing insight, and continuation analysis. B emphasized the missing operational connection and the contrary protective result. Neither reviewer requires every defense to fail or a new defense as a condition of contribution. These reviews guide the author's new standalone submission; they are not a rebuttal checklist or an instruction to discard the original contributions. A new finding in the IEEE S&P paper may be a valid original finding first obtained for the rejected manuscript.

| Review item | Obligation in the active plan | Required evidence | Present status |
|---|---|---|---|
| A-R1: incremental concept; DRIFT/ACE | Establish the external distinction of the retained controlled audit or the selected mechanism beyond known authority separation | Claim-specific nearest-method/artifact comparison and the evidence required for that empirical or mechanism claim | External novelty assessed per claim; own original findings retained |
| A-R2: 24 cases versus broad framing | Match claim breadth to independent structural evidence | Provenance, selection funnel, fresh workflows, uncertainty | Expansion planned |
| A-R3: disconnected studies | Join one task from exposure to committed effect | Operative per-run chain and mechanism-specific comparisons | Canonical payment and adapted native-email chains audited; autonomous continuity harm and controlled recovery reversal now observed; matched explanation/source fidelity and any claim-required source bridge remain bounded |
| A-R4: compressed terms and property | Make permission and the causal contrast assessable | Worked contract, clear denominators and figures | Definitions supplied; manuscript remains |
| A-R5: reconstruction | Bound inferential authority by implementation fidelity | Native conformance or explicit reconstruction claims | Per-system qualification required |
| B-R1: no sufficient new knowledge | Explain what original knowledge the retained and additional findings establish relative to external work | Valid empirical distinction, consequential mechanism or changed conclusion; predictive tests where claimed | Retained findings available at their scope; stronger claims conditional |
| B-R2: small selected populations | Establish consequence and bounded transfer | Realistic simulated effects, heterogeneous structures, explicit population | No deployment prevalence claim |
| B-R3: signal lacks consequences | Connect the actual intervention to native safety/utility/cost | Same-workflow outcomes and decision dependence | Payment and occurrence cohorts plus targeted contexts are retained; reconstructed-reference decisions and native sent effects observed; original-feedback parity and prospective causal/structural tests pending |
| B-R4: AttriGuard protects | Explain protection and any cost honestly | Corrected labels, protected checkpoints, causal comparisons | Contrary evidence retained |
| B-R5: continuous validation absent | Show that the measured mechanism drives the action | Valid IDs, actual effects, reachable states, same-state intervention | 236-run payment cohort: 22 violations; fresh occurrence core: 20; separate controlled L3: two. No pooling; independent human review and matched causal/structural claims remain pending |
| B-R6: version/reproduction limits | State scope for every implementation and comparison | Exact code/configuration, adapter diff, faithful contrasting method | Conditional work |

The follow-up review questions also require transparent selection/exclusion, distinctions between common assumptions and version-specific behavior, and readable figure hierarchy. The submission's ethics/open-science flags are not additional rejection reasons. Reporting corrections discovered independently remain obligations even where reviewers did not mention them.

Selected accepted-paper readings support precise scope, concrete boundaries, valid controls, visible counterevidence, and useful new knowledge. They do not identify ingredients that cause acceptance. In particular, predicting a permission preference is different from enforcing it; reducing capabilities is different from preserving all legitimate functionality; and detecting a gadget is different from verifying a complete consequence. Use the reading ledger in Appendix D without treating accepted status as validation of every assumption.

### 15.2 Reviewer-to-evidence map

| Reviewer concern | Retained author study | What this submission must establish | Decisive evidence or experiment |
|---|---|---|---|
| **A1, B1 — incremental concept / DRIFT and ACE overlap** | Correctly showed source-dependent signals; the generic principle “influence is not authority” was already familiar | A **specific** previously uncharacterized **operative reference/effect boundary**, or a scientifically distinctive controlled audit | Read and compare *actual method/guarantee*, then demonstrate source-bound reference-generation and true effect consequences; pivot if only a missing standard permission check remains |
| **A2, B2 — small, selected population** | 24 matched cases from four families, 14 live tasks; limited prevalence basis | Bounded significance on genuinely different effect semantics or honest narrow affected-version impact | Independent native operation; a **different** effect structure if warranted; log enrollment/exclusions; outcome-blind predictions; no ID-renaming as transfer |
| **A3 — disconnected defense experiments** | Multiple defenses/models/metrics but no same-path link | One actual trace from carrier → actor → shadow → release → dispatch → commit, and a causal intervention on the relevant component | Full event joins in identical supported execution; do not claim A14 scores directly caused payment outcomes without a bridge |
| **A4 — compressed writing/undefined property** | Dense terminology; “protected property” left implicit | A concrete one-use payment or scoped share contract that a reviewer can evaluate | Early worked case, defined legal/forbidden effects and appropriate deferrals, readable operation timeline and denominator labels |
| **A5, B6 — reconstruction and version fidelity** | Reconstructed CausalArmor scoring; AttriGuard results scoped to selected integration | Exact code/version/interface and source-faithful conditions, with adapter effects isolated | Byte hashes and source diff, actual native `FunctionsRuntime`, supported tool semantics, native benchmark qualification, separate label for reconstructions |
| **B3 — source/signal does not prove harm** | Measured attribution score movement and conditional judgments | A matched, **actual committed forbidden effect** causally influenced by reference evidence | Freeze valid actor candidate and world; manipulate only shadow evidence; measure review and independently committed effect under original route |
| **B4 — existing defense is protective** | Selected ALT outcome **17/70 OFF → 2/70 ON** under conflict | Explain **which step** protects, where that protection stops and whether required legal work survives | Preserve protected examples; grant gate as upper bound; clean/attacked comparisons; no “more rejection means more security” shortcut |
| **B5 — no continuous validation chain** | Different detection, routing, recovery and replay populations | Complete authorization/effect chain **in the same native workflow** | Occurrence IDs, actual shadow requests, current grant/state, dispatch/receipt/effect joins, matched native candidate intervention |
| **B2/B6 — external relevance and generalization** | Small constructed tasks and version-specific integrations | Precisely bounded transfer or impactful source-specific vulnerability | Native-supported second operation and independent structure as needed, not model-count inflation |
| **Reviewer A praise — controlled permissions** | Held value/action/effect/permission fixed across source placement | Preserve the scientific virtue of interventions that **do not covertly change authorization** | Fact-preserving, same-candidate shadow treatment; grant contrasts explicitly treated as *different* experiments |
| **Reviewer A praise — recovery** | Followed blocked paths and measured selected successful continuations | Joint secure completion, legal recovery, cost and any-time violations | Continue lifecycle; later success never erases an earlier forbidden commit |

### 15.3 Claims should advance with their evidence

Source observation supports a candidate mechanism. Faithful component reproduction supports route behavior. Autonomous reachability supports exposure under the stated attacker. Independent effect evidence supports a scoped violation/protection. A valid intervention supports the selected causal explanation. Fresh discriminating structures, nearest-work comparison and bounded relevance support the eventual contribution. These are different proof obligations, not an automatic venue qualification ladder.

An exploit witness establishes existence under its conditions; a rate needs a denominator and sampling model. A strong native scoped vulnerability can matter without a universal theory. A protective explanation requires a new predictive condition. A formal guarantee requires an explicit abstraction and evidence that the actual adapters/executor satisfy it; adding a toy theorem is insufficient.

The decisive displays are one fully worked contract-to-effect trace; a treatment/threat-specific enrollment and reachability table; a valid mechanism contrast; joint security/usefulness outcomes including the strongest protected case; and supported transfer/comparator/cost results. Every important number must map to primitive IDs and its own denominator. Preserve a readable main-text argument rather than a chronology of all accumulated studies.

### 15.4 Current argument and skeptical alternatives

The strongest candidate story concerns authorization of continuations, not a universal defect in all reference methods: agreement can preserve operation identity while failing prefix-relative amount/count or recipient-wise constraints; locally stricter admission can change the subsequent execution. Evidence must separate bypass, infeasible reference, permissive substitution and legitimate collective alternatives. The latter recipient-wise email transfer is planned, not observed.

A novelty reviewer can still call this familiar resource accounting; a causality reviewer can still note release/receipt bundles and selected prefixes; a rigor reviewer can cite receipt adaptation and failed functionality; a breadth reviewer can cite two joint configurations and authored simulators; a scientific-value reviewer can ask which explanation predicts protected cases. Section 7's interventions and the retained I/N, email-AFTER and historical protection counterexamples address those different objections without assuming they all disappear. A simple repair need not be a novel architecture, and a known principle can still expose an important concrete implementation consequence.

The historical forced gate blocks 113/240 AUTH and 87/240 ALT. The reported paired ALT−AUTH difference −0.1083 has interval [−0.3625, +0.1458]. Natural taxonomy uses lexical argument coverage, not semantic authority; long bodies can dominate short critical identifiers. Its population conditions on successful utility, mapped single calls, eligible spans, and scoring. Use a selection funnel and semantic review if reused. ### A.4 Artifact checks and their limits

The original inventory contains 5,788 files, including 1,056 Python files and 86 shell scripts, with 522 distinct Python/shell content hashes. Duplicated upstream/historical trees account for much of the size. Recorded checks report 56 frozen-claim passes, 5,787 integrity-verified files, 5,694 of 5,731 source files covered with 37 Git metadata exclusions, passing structural preflight/dry run, no recorded AST syntax failures, and 86 passing shell syntax checks.

These checks establish bounded packaging, stored-output, and syntax properties. They survived meaningful URL, capacity, and fresh/frozen defects, so they cannot substitute for execution validity. Relevant scientific pathways were traced and inspected, but not every vendored dependency was manually line-reviewed. No fresh GPU reproduction of those archival results is established by this plan.

### A.5 Reproduced primitive counterexamples and their limits

These lightweight offline checks concern the supplied artifact. They made no model calls and are recorded as component evidence. The oracle checks used `implementation_evidence/paef_oracle.py`; the isolated method came from the source-bound AttriGuard integration identified in Appendices A.3 and D.

| Primitive check | Observed result in the prior audit | What it establishes and what remains unknown |
|---|---|---|
| `_norm_url('https://example.test/A')` versus the lowercase-path URL | Equal normalized strings | A potentially meaningful URL-path distinction is collapsed; no live URL exploit or deployment frequency is demonstrated |
| Summary predicate on “The unemployment report is false: it is not 7.2 percent.” | Accepted | Keyword presence does not validate the requested summary's meaning; the frequency of incorrect source-specific labels is unknown |
| Hobbies predicate on “Alice dislikes painting; Charlie never goes swimming; Eve hates hiking.” | Accepted | Names and nearby hobbies do not validate the stated relationships; task correctness and effect authorization need separate predicates |
| Extracted `_fuzzy_survive` with an exact candidate and an existing judge reason | Returns true and preserves the earlier reason | Isolated telemetry persistence; neither native reachability nor an incorrect security verdict was demonstrated |

Use these examples to design semantic adjudication and primitive recomputation, rather than to infer a revised benchmark-wide rate. Test an incorrect security decision through the actual native route before claiming that stale explanatory text changes enforcement. A repaired new pilot does not retroactively repair the original immutable results.

### A.6 Skipped-call populations and the missing call-level join

The later inspection found **qualifying state in 44/210 defended runs**, **later use in 22/210 runs**, and **18/168 privileged calls without the ordinary audit**. These are three different denominators and stages, not interchangeable rates or a funnel with implied causal attribution.

The historical association labels those eighteen calls by their containing run's final outcome: **15 authorized, two selected unauthorized, and one neither**. A final-run label is not necessarily an immediate-call authorization verdict. It does not establish that eighteen unauthorized calls bypassed enforcement, that the two selected outcomes were caused by this route, or that a live attacker can induce it.

Before an approval-coverage claim, rebuild the call-level join: qualifying state and antecedent output/block/error → assessed proposal and approval/exemption → exact dispatch/call ID and arguments → actual immediate and partial effects → applicable permission at effect → later continuation and final outcome. Preserve the existing descriptive counts separately from newly adjudicated counts. Prove reachability under the stated attacker and source-bound version; include successful compensation and protected calls. Those historical final-run labels remain insufficient for call-level conclusions. The later fresh campaign separately supplies two joined autonomous harmful retries; it does not retroactively adjudicate these 18 calls or create an exploit rate.
